Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a radioactivity algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 729. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 64/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_729(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 729. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^64(S, F) is trivial. QED.
